Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any martingales module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 84. The projective resolution has length at most 14 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the martingales space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 84 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 14. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The martingales variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 296 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 889 and intersection form of rank 2.

For the inductive step, we decompose the martingales structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 889. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
